import { Navigate, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom'
import PortfolioPage from './pages/PortfolioPage'
import AdminPage from './pages/AdminPage'

export default function App() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/" element={<PortfolioPage />} />
      <Route path="/admin" element={<AdminPage />} />
      <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
    </Routes>
  )
}

src/admin/BlockEditor.jsx - Content:
import { useState } from 'react'

function parseItems(value) {
  return value.split('\n').map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean)
}

function projectText(items) {
  return (items || []).map((item) => {
    return `${item.name}\n${item.summary}\n${item.resources}\n${item.usage}`
  }).join('\n\n---\n\n')
}

function parseProjects(value) {
  const chunks = value.split('\n\n---\n\n').map((part) => part.trim()).filter(Boolean)
  return chunks.map((chunk) => {
    const lines = chunk.split('\n')
    return {
      name: lines[0] || '',
      summary: lines[1] || '',
      resources: lines[2] || '',
      usage: lines.slice(3).join(' ') || '',
    }
  })
}

export default function BlockEditor({ blocks, lang, onChange }) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(0)
  const block = blocks[selected]
  const data = block?.content?.[lang] || {}

  function updateField(name, value) {
    const next = blocks.map((entry, index) => {
      if (index !== selected) {
        return entry
      }
      return {
        ...entry,
        content: {
          ...entry.content,
          [lang]: {
            ...entry.content?.[lang],
            [name]: value,
          },
        },
      }
    })
    onChange(next)
  }

  return (
    <div className="editor-grid">
      <aside>
        <h3>Blocks</h3>
        <ul className="editor-list">
          {blocks.map((entry, index) => (
            <li key={`${entry.type}-${index}`}>
              <button type="button" className={index === selected ? 'active' : ''} onClick={() => setSelected(index)}>{entry.type}</button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </aside>

      <section>
        <h3>{block?.type} ({lang})</h3>
        {block?.type === 'headline' && (
          <textarea value={data.title || ''} onChange={(event) => updateField('title', event.target.value)} />
        )}
        {block?.type === 'about' && (
          <>
            <input value={data.title || ''} onChange={(event) => updateField('title', event.target.value)} />
            <textarea value={data.text || ''} onChange={(event) => updateField('text', event.target.value)} />
          </>
        )}
        {(block?.type === 'experience' || block?.type === 'certifications') && (
          <>
            <input value={data.title || ''} onChange={(event) => updateField('title', event.target.value)} />
            <textarea value={(data.items || []).join('\n')} onChange={(event) => updateField('items', parseItems(event.target.value))} />
          </>
        )}
        {block?.type === 'projects' && (
          <>
            <input value={data.title || ''} onChange={(event) => updateField('title', event.target.value)} />
            <textarea value={projectText(data.items)} onChange={(event) => updateField('items', parseProjects(event.target.value))} />
          </>
        )}
        {block?.type === 'contact' && (
          <>
            <input value={data.title || ''} onChange={(event) => updateField('title', event.target.value)} />
            <textarea value={data.cta || ''} onChange={(event) => updateField('cta', event.target.value)} />
          </>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  )
}

src/assets/i18n.js - Content:
/*
  Minimal runtime i18n for static pages.

  Usage:
    1) Add translations to window.__I18N__ = { en: {...}, pt: {...}, de: {...}, es: {...} }
    2) Add language buttons with .lang-btn[data-lang="en|pt|de|es"]
    3) Mark nodes:
       - data-i18n="key"       (textContent)
       - data-i18n-html="key"  (innerHTML)
*/

const STORAGE_KEY = "pablobhz_lang"
const SUPPORTED_LANGS = ["en", "pt", "de", "es"]

function normalizeLang(input) {
  const v = String(input || "").toLowerCase()

  if (v.startsWith("pt")) {
    return "pt"
  }
  if (v.startsWith("de")) {
    return "de"
  }
  if (v.startsWith("es")) {
    return "es"
  }
  return "en"
}

function pickInitialLang() {
  const stored = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY)
  if (stored) {
    return normalizeLang(stored)
  }
  return normalizeLang(navigator.language)
}

function applyTranslations(dict, lang) {
  document.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n]").forEach(function (el) {
    const k = el.dataset.i18n
    if (k && dict[lang] && dict[lang][k] != null) {
      el.textContent = dict[lang][k]
    }
  })

  document.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n-html]").forEach(function (el) {
    const k = el.dataset.i18nHtml
    if (k && dict[lang] && dict[lang][k] != null) {
      el.innerHTML = dict[lang][k]
    }
  })
}

function updateHtmlLang(lang) {
  if (lang === "pt") {
    document.documentElement.lang = "pt-BR"
  } else if (lang === "de") {
    document.documentElement.lang = "de"
  } else if (lang === "es") {
    document.documentElement.lang = "es"
  } else {
    document.documentElement.lang = "en"
  }
}

export function setLang(lang) {
  const dict = window.__I18N__ || {}
  const next = normalizeLang(lang)

  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, next)
  updateHtmlLang(next)

  document.querySelectorAll(".lang-btn").forEach(function (btn) {
    const isActive = normalizeLang(btn.dataset.lang) === next
    btn.classList.toggle("active", isActive)
  })

  applyTranslations(dict, next)
}

export function initI18n() {
  const dict = window.__I18N__ || {}
  const missing = SUPPORTED_LANGS.filter(function (l) {
    return !dict[l]
  })

  if (missing.length > 0) {
    console.warn("[i18n] window.__I18N__ missing dictionaries for: " + missing.join(", "))
  }

  document.querySelectorAll(".lang-btn").forEach(function (btn) {
    btn.addEventListener("click", function () {
      setLang(btn.dataset.lang)
    })
  })

  setLang(pickInitialLang())
}

// Convenience for inline scripts without module imports
window.PABLO_I18N = { initI18n, setLang }

src/cms/api.js - Content:
import { fetchAuthSession, signIn, signOut } from 'aws-amplify/auth'
import { appConfig } from '../lib/config'
import { seedContent } from './seedContent'

async function authHeader() {
  const session = await fetchAuthSession()
  const token = session.tokens?.idToken?.toString()
  if (!token) {
    throw new Error('Not authenticated')
  }
  return { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
}

async function callApi(path, options = {}, useAuth = false) {
  if (!appConfig.apiUrl) {
    return null
  }

  const headers = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
  if (useAuth) {
    Object.assign(headers, await authHeader())
  }

  const response = await fetch(`${appConfig.apiUrl}${path}`, {
    ...options,
    headers: { ...headers, ...(options.headers || {}) },
  })

  if (!response.ok) {
    const payload = await response.json().catch(() => ({}))
    throw new Error(payload.message || `HTTP ${response.status}`)
  }

  return response.json()
}

export async function loadPublicPage(lang) {
  const data = await callApi(`/public/page/home?lang=${encodeURIComponent(lang)}`)
  if (!data) {
    return {
      settings: seedContent.settings,
      page: seedContent.page,
    }
  }
  return data
}

export async function adminLogin(username, password) {
  return signIn({ username, password })
}

export async function adminLogout() {
  return signOut()
}

export async function loadAdminData() {
  const [page, settings, fonts] = await Promise.all([
    callApi('/admin/pages/home', {}, true),
    callApi('/admin/settings', {}, true),
    callApi('/admin/fonts', {}, true).catch(() => []),
  ])

  return {
    page: page || seedContent.page,
    settings: settings || seedContent.settings,
    fonts: fonts || [],
  }
}

export async function saveAdminData(pageId, blocks, settings) {
  if (!appConfig.apiUrl) {
    return
  }

  await Promise.all([
    callApi(`/admin/pages/${pageId}`, {
      method: 'PUT',
      body: JSON.stringify({ blocks }),
    }, true),
    callApi('/admin/settings', {
      method: 'PUT',
      body: JSON.stringify(settings),
    }, true),
  ])
}

src/components/BlockRenderer.jsx - Content:
function textFor(block, lang) {
  return block.content?.[lang] || block.content?.en || {}
}

function renderSimpleList(items) {
  return (
    <ul>
      {items.map((item) => <li key={item}>{item}</li>)}
    </ul>
  )
}

function renderProjectList(items, lang) {
  const labelResources = lang === 'pt-BR' ? 'Recursos usados' : 'Resources used'
  const labelUsage = lang === 'pt-BR' ? 'Como foram usados' : 'How they were used'

  return (
    <div className="project-list">
      {items.map((item) => (
        <article key={item.name} className="project-item">
          <h4>{item.name}</h4>
          <p>{item.summary}</p>
          <p><strong>{labelResources}:</strong> {item.resources}</p>
          <p><strong>{labelUsage}:</strong> {item.usage}</p>
        </article>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

export default function BlockRenderer({ block, lang }) {
  const data = textFor(block, lang)

  if (block.type === 'headline') {
    return <section className="section section-hero"><h2>{data.title}</h2></section>
  }

  if (block.type === 'about') {
    return (
      <section className="section">
        <h3>{data.title}</h3>
        <p>{data.text}</p>
      </section>
    )
  }

  if (block.type === 'experience' || block.type === 'certifications') {
    return (
      <section className="section">
        <h3>{data.title}</h3>
        {renderSimpleList(data.items || [])}
      </section>
    )
  }

  if (block.type === 'projects') {
    return (
      <section className="section">
        <h3>{data.title}</h3>
        {renderProjectList(data.items || [], lang)}
      </section>
    )
  }

  if (block.type === 'contact') {
    return (
      <section className="section">
        <h3>{data.title}</h3>
        <p>{data.cta}</p>
      </section>
    )
  }

  return null
}

src/components/Sidebar.jsx - Content:
export default function Sidebar({ settings, lang, onLangChange }) {
  const role = settings.role?.[lang] || settings.role?.en || ''

  return (
    <aside className="sidebar">
      <div className="profile">
        <div className="avatar" aria-label="Profile picture placeholder">
          {settings.profileImageUrl ? <img src={settings.profileImageUrl} alt={settings.fullName} /> : <span>{settings.profileInitials || 'PC'}</span>}
        </div>
        <h1>{settings.fullName}</h1>
        <p>{role}</p>
      </div>

      <div className="lang-switch">
        <button type="button" className={lang === 'en' ? 'active' : ''} onClick={() => onLangChange('en')}>EN</button>
        <button type="button" className={lang === 'pt-BR' ? 'active' : ''} onClick={() => onLangChange('pt-BR')}>PT-BR</button>
      </div>

      <ul className="meta">
        <li>{settings.location}</li>
        <li><a href={settings.contacts?.linkedin} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">LinkedIn</a></li>
        <li><a href={settings.contacts?.github} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">GitHub</a></li>
        <li><a href={`mailto:${settings.contacts?.email}`}>{settings.contacts?.email}</a></li>
      </ul>
    </aside>
  )
}

src/lib/i18n.js - Content:
const KEY = 'lang'
export const SUPPORTED_LANGS = ['en', 'pt-BR']

export function normalizeLang(input) {
  const value = String(input || '').toLowerCase()
  if (value.startsWith('pt')) {
    return 'pt-BR'
  }
  return 'en'
}

export function getInitialLang() {
  const stored = localStorage.getItem(KEY)
  if (stored) {
    return normalizeLang(stored)
  }
  return normalizeLang(navigator.language)
}

export function persistLang(lang) {
  localStorage.setItem(KEY, normalizeLang(lang))
}

import React from 'react'
import ReactDOM from 'react-dom/client'
import { BrowserRouter } from 'react-router-dom'
import { Amplify } from 'aws-amplify'
import App from './App'
import { appConfig } from './lib/config'
import './styles/global.css'

if (appConfig.cognito.userPoolId && appConfig.cognito.userPoolClientId) {
  Amplify.configure({
    Auth: {
      Cognito: {
        userPoolId: appConfig.cognito.userPoolId,
        userPoolClientId: appConfig.cognito.userPoolClientId,
      },
    },
  })
}

ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById('root')).render(
  <React.StrictMode>
    <BrowserRouter>
      <App />
    </BrowserRouter>
  </React.StrictMode>
)

src/pages/AdminPage.jsx - Content:
import { useState } from 'react'
import BlockEditor from '../admin/BlockEditor'
import { adminLogin, adminLogout, loadAdminData, saveAdminData } from '../cms/api'
import { seedContent } from '../cms/seedContent'

export default function AdminPage() {
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [authed, setAuthed] = useState(false)
  const [lang, setLang] = useState('en')
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState(seedContent.settings)
  const [blocks, setBlocks] = useState(seedContent.page.blocks)
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('')

  async function handleLogin(event) {
    event.preventDefault()
    setStatus('Signing in...')
    try {
      await adminLogin(username, password)
      const data = await loadAdminData().catch(() => ({ page: seedContent.page, settings: seedContent.settings }))
      setSettings(data.settings)
      setBlocks(data.page.blocks || [])
      setAuthed(true)
      setStatus('')
    } catch {
      setStatus('Authentication failed. If backend is not configured, use seeded content mode.')
    }
  }

  async function handleSave() {
    setStatus('Saving...')
    try {
      await saveAdminData('home', blocks, settings)
      setStatus('Saved.')
    } catch {
      setStatus('Saved locally only (API not configured).')
    }
  }

  async function handleLogout() {
    await adminLogout().catch(() => null)
    setAuthed(false)
  }

  if (!authed) {
    return (
      <main className="admin-auth">
        <form onSubmit={handleLogin}>
          <h2>CMS Admin</h2>
          <input placeholder="Username" value={username} onChange={(event) => setUsername(event.target.value)} />
          <input placeholder="Password" type="password" value={password} onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)} />
          <button type="submit">Sign in</button>
          {status && <p>{status}</p>}
        </form>
      </main>
    )
  }

  return (
    <main className="admin-page">
      <header>
        <h2>Portfolio CMS</h2>
        <div>
          <button type="button" className={lang === 'en' ? 'active' : ''} onClick={() => setLang('en')}>EN</button>
          <button type="button" className={lang === 'pt-BR' ? 'active' : ''} onClick={() => setLang('pt-BR')}>PT-BR</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={handleSave}>Save</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={handleLogout}>Logout</button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <section className="settings-panel">
        <h3>Site settings</h3>
        <input
          placeholder="Profile image URL"
          value={settings.profileImageUrl || ''}
          onChange={(event) => setSettings({ ...settings, profileImageUrl: event.target.value })}
        />
      </section>

      <BlockEditor blocks={blocks} lang={lang} onChange={setBlocks} />
      {status && <p className="status-line">{status}</p>}
    </main>
  )
}

const FILTERS = [
  { key: 'all', category: null },
  { key: 'certifications', category: 'certifications' },
  { key: 'projects', category: 'projects' },
  { key: 'badges', category: 'badges' },
]

const CATEGORY_EMOJI = {
  projects: '🚀',
  certifications: '🏆',
  badges: '🤖',
}

const PortfolioPage = () => {
  const [lang, setLang] = useState('en')
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState('dark')
  const [activeView, setActiveView] = useState('about')
  const [activeFilter, setActiveFilter] = useState('all')
  const [expVisible, setExpVisible] = useState(1)
  const [eduVisible, setEduVisible] = useState(1)
  const [nfVisible, setNfVisible] = useState(1)
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ name: '', email: '', message: '' })

  useEffect(() => {
    const storedLang = localStorage.getItem(LANG_KEY)
    const storedTheme = localStorage.getItem(THEME_KEY)

    if (storedLang && DICTIONARY[storedLang]) {
      setLang(storedLang)
    }

    if (storedTheme === 'light' || storedTheme === 'dark') {
      setTheme(storedTheme)
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', storedTheme)
    } else {
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark')
    }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem(LANG_KEY, lang)
  }, [lang])

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem(THEME_KEY, theme)
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme)
  }, [theme])

  const copy = DICTIONARY[lang]

  const filterCounts = useMemo(() => {
    const items = copy.portfolio.items
    return {
      all: items.length,
      certifications: items.filter((item) => item.category === 'certifications').length,
      projects: items.filter((item) => item.category === 'projects').length,
      badges: items.filter((item) => item.category === 'badges').length,
    }
  }, [copy.portfolio.items])

  const filteredPortfolio = useMemo(() => {
    const selected = FILTERS.find((filter) => filter.key === activeFilter)
    if (!selected || !selected.category) {
      return copy.portfolio.items
    }

    return copy.portfolio.items.filter((item) => item.category === selected.category)
  }, [activeFilter, copy.portfolio.items])

  const openMailto = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault()

    const subject = encodeURIComponent(copy.ui.messageSubject)
    const body = encodeURIComponent(
      `Name: ${formData.name}\nEmail: ${formData.email}\n\n${formData.message}`,
    )

    window.location.href = `mailto:${copy.profile.email}?subject=${subject}&body=${body}`
  }

  const sectionTitle = (title) => (
    <h2 className="section-title">
      {title}
      <span>{copy.ui.sectionDot}</span>
    </h2>
  )

  return (
    <div className="page-shell">
      <div className="layout-wrap">
        <aside className="sidebar">
          <nav className="side-nav">
            {NAV_ITEMS.map((view) => (
              <button
                key={view}
                type="button"
                className={activeView === view ? 'active' : ''}
                onClick={() => setActiveView(view)}
              >
                {copy.nav[view]}
              </button>
            ))}
          </nav>

          <div className="side-controls">
            <input type="search" placeholder={copy.ui.searchPlaceholder} aria-label={copy.ui.searchPlaceholder} />

            <div className="lang-switch" role="group" aria-label={copy.ui.language}>
              <button type="button" className={lang === 'en' ? 'active' : ''} onClick={() => setLang('en')}>
                EN
              </button>
              <button type="button" className={lang === 'pt-BR' ? 'active' : ''} onClick={() => setLang('pt-BR')}>
                PT
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="theme-switch" role="group" aria-label="Theme">
              <button type="button" className={theme === 'dark' ? 'active' : ''} onClick={() => setTheme('dark')}>
                {copy.ui.dark}
              </button>
              <button type="button" className={theme === 'light' ? 'active' : ''} onClick={() => setTheme('light')}>
                {copy.ui.light}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </aside>

        <main className="main-content">
          <header className="profile-banner">
            <div className="banner-main">
              <div className="profile-image-wrap">
                {/* TODO: place Pablo-Profile.jpeg at public/profile.jpeg */}
                <img src="/profile.jpeg" alt={copy.profile.name} className="profile-image" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="status-pill">{copy.ui.available}</p>
                <h1>{copy.profile.name}</h1>
                <p className="banner-role">{copy.profile.role}</p>
                <p className="banner-description">{copy.profile.aboutStatus}</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="banner-meta">
              <p>{copy.profile.location}</p>
              <a href={`mailto:${copy.profile.email}`}>{copy.profile.email}</a>
              <a href={copy.profile.cvUrl} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
                {copy.ui.downloadCv}
              </a>
              <div className="social-links">
                <a href={copy.profile.linkedin} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" aria-label="LinkedIn">
                  in
                </a>
                <a href={copy.profile.github} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" aria-label="GitHub">
                  gh
                </a>
              </div>
            </div>
          </header>

          <section key={activeView} className="view-panel view-fade">
            {activeView === 'about' && (
              <div className="view-grid">
                <article className="card about-card">
                  {sectionTitle(copy.about.title)}
                  {copy.about.paragraphs.map((paragraph) => (
                    <p key={paragraph}>{paragraph}</p>
                  ))}
                </article>

                <div className="stats-grid">
                  {copy.about.stats.map((stat) => (
                    <article key={stat.label} className="stat-card">
                      <strong>{stat.value}</strong>
                      <span>{stat.label}</span>
                    </article>
                  ))}
                </div>

                <article className="card">
                  <h3>{copy.ui.whatIDoTitle}</h3>
                  <div className="services-grid">
                    {copy.about.services.map((service) => (
                      <article key={service.title} className="service-card">
                        <h4>{service.title}</h4>
                        <p>{service.text}</p>
                      </article>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </article>

                <article className="card">
                  <h3>{copy.ui.stackTitle}</h3>
                  <div className="pill-wrap">
                    {copy.about.stack.map((item) => (
                      <span key={item} className="pill">
                        {item}
                      </span>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </article>

                <article className="card">
                  <h3>{copy.ui.clientsTitle}</h3>
                  <div className="clients-grid">
                    {copy.about.clients.map((client) => (
                      <div key={client} className="client-tile">
                        {client}
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </article>
              </div>
            )}

            {activeView === 'resume' && (
              <div className="view-grid">
                <article className="card">
                  {sectionTitle(copy.resume.title)}
                  <div className="timeline">
                    {copy.resume.experience.slice(0, expVisible).map((item) => (
                      <article key={`${item.role}-${item.period}`} className="timeline-item">
                        <span>{item.period}</span>
                        <h4>
                          {item.role} · {item.company}
                        </h4>
                        <p>{item.description}</p>
                      </article>
                    ))}
                  </div>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="ghost-btn"
                    onClick={() => setExpVisible(expVisible === 1 ? copy.resume.experience.length : 1)}
                  >
                    {expVisible === 1 ? copy.ui.showMore : copy.ui.showLess}
                  </button>
                </article>

                <article className="card split-sections">
                  <div>
                    <h3>{copy.ui.educationTitle}</h3>
                    <div className="timeline compact">
                      {copy.resume.education.slice(0, eduVisible).map((item) => (
                        <article key={`${item.title}-${item.period}`} className="timeline-item">
                          <span>{item.period}</span>
                          <h4>{item.title}</h4>
                          <p>{item.school}</p>
                        </article>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="ghost-btn"
                      onClick={() => setEduVisible(eduVisible === 1 ? copy.resume.education.length : 1)}
                    >
                      {eduVisible === 1 ? copy.ui.showMore : copy.ui.showLess}
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <h3>{copy.ui.nonformalEducationTitle}</h3>
                    <div className="timeline compact">
                      {copy.resume.nonformal.slice(0, nfVisible).map((item) => (
                        <article key={`${item.title}-${item.period}`} className="timeline-item">
                          <span>{item.period}</span>
                          <h4>{item.title}</h4>
                          <p>{item.school}</p>
                        </article>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="ghost-btn"
                      onClick={() => setNfVisible(nfVisible === 1 ? copy.resume.nonformal.length : 1)}
                    >
                      {nfVisible === 1 ? copy.ui.showMore : copy.ui.showLess}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </article>

                <article className="card">
                  <h3>{copy.ui.competencyTitle}</h3>
                  <div className="pill-wrap">
                    {copy.resume.competencies.map((item) => (
                      <span key={item} className="pill">
                        {item}
                      </span>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </article>

                <article className="card">
                  <h3>{copy.ui.toolsTitle}</h3>
                  <div className="tools-grid">
                    {copy.resume.tools.map((tool) => (
                      <article key={tool.name} className="tool-item">
                        <strong>{tool.name}</strong>
                        <span className={`level ${tool.level.toLowerCase()}`}>{tool.level}</span>
                      </article>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </article>
              </div>
            )}

            {activeView === 'portfolio' && (
              <div className="view-grid">
                <article className="card">
                  {sectionTitle(copy.portfolio.title)}
                  <div className="filter-row">
                    {FILTERS.map((filter) => (
                      <button
                        key={filter.key}
                        type="button"
                        className={activeFilter === filter.key ? 'active' : ''}
                        onClick={() => setActiveFilter(filter.key)}
                      >
                        {copy.ui[filter.key]} {filterCounts[filter.key]}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </article>

                <div className="portfolio-grid">
                  {filteredPortfolio.map((item) => (
                    <article key={item.id} className="portfolio-card">
                      <a href={item.href} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="portfolio-cover-link">
                        <div className={`portfolio-cover ${item.coverClass}`}>
                          <span className="category-badge">
                            {CATEGORY_EMOJI[item.category]} {copy.ui[item.category]}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </a>
                      <h4>{item.title}</h4>
                      <p>{item.description}</p>
                    </article>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {activeView === 'contact' && (
              <div className="view-grid">
                <article className="card">
                  {sectionTitle(copy.contact.title)}
                  <h3>{copy.ui.touchTitle}</h3>
                  <p>{copy.ui.touchText}</p>
                  <div className="contact-grid">
                    {copy.contact.cards.map((card) => (
                      <a key={card.label} href={card.href} className="contact-card" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
                        <span>{card.label}</span>
                        <strong>{card.value}</strong>
                      </a>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </article>

                <article className="card">
                  <form className="contact-form" onSubmit={openMailto}>
                    <input
                      required
                      type="text"
                      placeholder={copy.ui.formName}
                      value={formData.name}
                      onChange={(event) => setFormData({ ...formData, name: event.target.value })}
                    />
                    <input
                      required
                      type="email"
                      placeholder={copy.ui.formEmail}
                      value={formData.email}
                      onChange={(event) => setFormData({ ...formData, email: event.target.value })}
                    />
                    <textarea
                      required
                      rows={6}
                      placeholder={copy.ui.formMessage}
                      value={formData.message}
                      onChange={(event) => setFormData({ ...formData, message: event.target.value })}
                    />
                    <button type="submit" className="primary-btn">
                      {copy.ui.submit}
                    </button>
                  </form>
                </article>
              </div>
            )}
          </section>
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export default PortfolioPage

                <span class="font-label text-sm text-on-surface-variant opacity-30 shrink-0">03</span>

      <!-- ===== The Architecture ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-48">
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-16 items-start">
          <div class="md:col-span-5 md:sticky md:top-32">
            <h3 class="font-headline font-bold text-4xl mb-8 tracking-tight" data-i18n="blueprint_title">01 // The Architecture</h3>
            <p class="font-body text-lg text-on-surface-variant leading-loose mb-12" data-i18n="blueprint_desc">
              Cosmo is built serverless-first on AWS. A React/TypeScript SPA communicates with a suite of AWS Lambda functions behind API Gateway. DynamoDB stores user profiles, workout history, and achievement data. AWS Cognito handles authentication. AWS Bedrock (Nova Pro) generates AI workout plans from user inputs — goals, fitness level, available equipment.
            </p>
            <div class="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-secondary shrink-0"></span>
                <span class="font-label text-sm uppercase tracking-wider" data-i18n="bp_1">AI Workout Generation via Bedrock</span>
              </div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-primary shrink-0"></span>
                <span class="font-label text-sm uppercase tracking-wider" data-i18n="bp_2">Serverless Lambda + DynamoDB Backend</span>
              </div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-outline shrink-0"></span>
                <span class="font-label text-sm uppercase tracking-wider" data-i18n="bp_3">Cognito JWT Authentication</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="md:col-span-7">
            <!-- Architecture visual placeholder — no screenshots exist for Cosmo -->
            <div class="bg-ink-700 relative overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center" style="border-radius: 0.5rem; min-height: 400px;">
              <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-cyan-arc opacity-[0.12]" style="font-size: 14rem;">fitness_center</span>
              <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-ink-700 via-transparent to-transparent"></div>

                <span class="font-label text-[10px] text-secondary font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-2" data-i18n="blueprint_overlay_label">System Overview</span>
                <div class="flex justify-between items-end">
                  <p class="font-body text-xs text-on-surface max-w-[220px]" data-i18n="blueprint_overlay_desc">React SPA → API Gateway → Lambda → DynamoDB + Bedrock Nova Pro</p>
                  <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-secondary">settings_input_component</span>

      <!-- ===== The Challenge ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-48">
        <div class="bg-ink-700 p-8 md:p-24 relative overflow-hidden">
          <div class="absolute -top-12 -right-8 opacity-[0.04] font-headline font-black text-[10rem] select-none hidden md:block">AI</div>
          <div class="max-w-3xl">
            <h3 class="font-headline font-bold text-4xl mb-12 tracking-tight" data-i18n="challenge_title">02 // The AI Integration Challenge</h3>
            <p class="font-body text-xl text-on-surface-variant leading-loose mb-16" data-i18n="challenge_desc">
              Integrating a large language model into a fitness app requires more than an API call. The prompt engineering needed to produce safe, structured, periodized workout plans — with correct sets, reps, and progressive overload — took significant iteration. The output also needed to be parsed reliably into a data schema the frontend could render, not just raw text.
            </p>
            <div class="code-insight">
              <div class="code-insight-header">
                <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-secondary text-sm">terminal</span>
                <span class="code-insight-label" data-i18n="insight_label">Engineering Insight</span>
              </div>
              <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant italic" data-i18n="insight_text">
                "The hardest part wasn't the AWS integration — it was making the LLM output consistent enough that the UI could render it deterministically."
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ===== Key Decisions ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-48">
        <h3 class="font-headline font-bold text-4xl mb-16 tracking-tight" data-i18n="decisions_title">03 // Key Engineering Decisions</h3>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-px bg-outline-variant/20">
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-ink-800 transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d1_kicker">AI Layer</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d1_title">Bedrock + Nova Pro</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d1_desc">
              AWS Bedrock abstracts the model provider. Nova Pro was chosen for its structured output support — critical for generating machine-readable workout JSON from natural language prompts without post-processing hacks.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-ink-800 transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d2_kicker">Auth Strategy</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d2_title">Cognito Identity Pools</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d2_desc">
              AWS Cognito handles user sign-up, sign-in, and JWT issuance. Lambda functions validate tokens server-side — no custom auth logic required. The same auth layer serves both the web app and the iOS companion app.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-ink-800 transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d3_kicker">Data Model</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d3_title">DynamoDB Single-Table</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d3_desc">
              A single DynamoDB table with GSI access patterns covers users, workout history, achievements, and calendar entries. Pay-per-request billing fits the usage profile of a fitness app — spiky, not constant.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-ink-800 transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d4_kicker">Mobile Companion</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d4_title">iOS + SwiftUI</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d4_desc">
              COSMOFIT (iOS 16+, SwiftUI) is a companion app sharing the same AWS backend. Puppeteer-based E2E tests cover the web flows; native iOS testing (XCTest) covers the mobile layer separately.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ===== WhatsApp Feedback ===== -->

              <span data-i18n="hero_subtitle">Pipeline.</span>
            </h1>
            <p class="font-body text-xl md:text-2xl text-on-surface-variant max-w-2xl leading-relaxed" data-i18n="hero_desc">

        <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8">
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-16">
            <div class="flex flex-col">
              <span class="metric-label" data-i18n="metric_1_label">Ingestion</span>
              <h2 class="metric-value text-5xl md:text-6xl" data-i18n="metric_1_value">Event-Driven</h2>
              <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant mt-4 text-sm max-w-[220px]" data-i18n="metric_1_desc">S3 object creation events trigger the Lambda automatically — no polling, no scheduler, no idle compute cost.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-col">
              <span class="metric-label" data-i18n="metric_2_label">Query Engine</span>
              <h2 class="metric-value text-5xl md:text-6xl" data-i18n="metric_2_value">Athena</h2>
              <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant mt-4 text-sm max-w-[220px]" data-i18n="metric_2_desc">AWS Athena queries the processed data with standard SQL directly on S3 — no database cluster to manage or scale.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-col">
              <span class="metric-label" data-i18n="metric_3_label">CI/CD</span>
              <h2 class="metric-value text-5xl md:text-6xl" data-i18n="metric_3_value">Actions</h2>
              <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant mt-4 text-sm max-w-[220px]" data-i18n="metric_3_desc">GitHub Actions automates Terraform plan and apply on every push — infrastructure changes are code-reviewed and auditable.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ===== The Architecture ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-48">
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-16 items-start">
          <div class="md:col-span-5 md:sticky md:top-32">
            <h3 class="font-headline font-bold text-4xl mb-8 tracking-tight" data-i18n="blueprint_title">01 // The Architecture</h3>
            <p class="font-body text-lg text-on-surface-variant leading-loose mb-12" data-i18n="blueprint_desc">
              The pipeline is entirely serverless. Raw data files (CSV, JSON, Parquet) are dropped into a raw S3 bucket. An S3 event triggers a Python Lambda that validates and routes the file. AWS Glue crawls the processed bucket, updates the Data Catalog, and runs ETL jobs to transform and partition the data. Athena queries the processed layer with SQL on demand.
            </p>
            <div class="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-secondary shrink-0"></span>
                <span class="font-label text-sm uppercase tracking-wider" data-i18n="bp_1">Event-Driven Ingestion (S3 → Lambda)</span>
              </div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-primary shrink-0"></span>
                <span class="font-label text-sm uppercase tracking-wider" data-i18n="bp_2">Glue Catalog + ETL Transformation</span>
              </div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-outline shrink-0"></span>
                <span class="font-label text-sm uppercase tracking-wider" data-i18n="bp_3">Athena SQL Queries on S3</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="md:col-span-7">
            <div class="bg-ink-700 relative overflow-hidden p-10" style="border-radius: 0.5rem; min-height: 400px;">
              <!-- Pipeline flow diagram -->
              <div class="flex flex-col gap-4">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                  <div class="w-12 h-12 bg-ink-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0" style="border-radius: 0.25rem;">
                    <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-secondary text-xl">upload_file</span>
                  </div>
                  <div class="h-px flex-1 bg-outline-variant/30"></div>
                  <div class="w-12 h-12 bg-ink-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0" style="border-radius: 0.25rem;">
                    <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-cyan-arc text-xl">storage</span>
                  </div>
                  <div class="h-px flex-1 bg-outline-variant/30"></div>
                  <div class="w-12 h-12 bg-ink-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0" style="border-radius: 0.25rem;">
                    <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary text-xl">functions</span>
                  </div>
                  <div class="ml-2">
                    <span class="font-label text-[9px] uppercase text-on-surface-variant">Raw S3 → Lambda trigger</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div class="ml-6 border-l-2 border-outline-variant/20 pl-6 flex flex-col gap-4 py-2">
                  <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                    <div class="w-12 h-12 bg-ink-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0" style="border-radius: 0.25rem;">
                      <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-secondary text-xl">schema</span>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <span class="font-label text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface block">AWS Glue</span>
                      <span class="font-label text-[9px] text-on-surface-variant">Crawl · Catalog · ETL transform</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                  <div class="w-12 h-12 bg-ink-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0" style="border-radius: 0.25rem;">
                    <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-cyan-arc text-xl">storage</span>
                  </div>
                  <div class="h-px flex-1 bg-outline-variant/30"></div>
                  <div class="w-12 h-12 bg-ink-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0" style="border-radius: 0.25rem;">
                    <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary text-xl">manage_search</span>
                  </div>
                  <div class="ml-2">
                    <span class="font-label text-[9px] uppercase text-on-surface-variant">Processed S3 → Athena SQL</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="absolute bottom-6 left-6 right-6 glass-effect p-4 border border-white/5" style="border-radius: 0.25rem;">
                <span class="font-label text-[10px] text-secondary font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-1" data-i18n="blueprint_overlay_label">Pipeline Overview</span>
                <p class="font-body text-xs text-on-surface-variant" data-i18n="blueprint_overlay_desc">S3 (raw) → Lambda → Glue ETL → S3 (processed) → Athena</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ===== The Challenge ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-48">
        <div class="bg-ink-700 p-8 md:p-24 relative overflow-hidden">
          <div class="absolute -top-12 -right-8 opacity-[0.04] font-headline font-black text-[10rem] select-none hidden md:block">ETL</div>
          <div class="max-w-3xl">
            <h3 class="font-headline font-bold text-4xl mb-12 tracking-tight" data-i18n="challenge_title">02 // The Schema Evolution Challenge</h3>
            <p class="font-body text-xl text-on-surface-variant leading-loose mb-16" data-i18n="challenge_desc">
              Raw data sources rarely deliver clean, consistent schemas. Files arrive with missing columns, changed types, or new fields that break downstream Glue jobs. The pipeline needs to handle schema drift gracefully — detecting changes, updating the Glue Catalog, and not dropping data mid-pipeline when source schemas evolve.
            </p>
            <div class="code-insight">
              <div class="code-insight-header">
                <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-secondary text-sm">terminal</span>
                <span class="code-insight-label" data-i18n="insight_label">Engineering Insight</span>
              </div>
              <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant italic" data-i18n="insight_text">
                "The pipeline's job isn't just to move data — it's to guarantee that the data arriving at Athena is always in a known, queryable shape, regardless of what the source throws at you."
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ===== Key Decisions ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-48">
        <h3 class="font-headline font-bold text-4xl mb-16 tracking-tight" data-i18n="decisions_title">03 // Key Engineering Decisions</h3>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-px bg-outline-variant/20">
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-ink-800 transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d1_kicker">Ingestion Pattern</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d1_title">S3 Event → Lambda (no scheduler)</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d1_desc">
              Files trigger the pipeline the moment they land in S3 — no cron jobs, no polling. Lambda runs only when there's work. This eliminates idle compute cost and makes latency between arrival and processing near-zero, while keeping the architecture entirely event-driven.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-ink-800 transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d2_kicker">Transformation</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d2_title">AWS Glue for ETL</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d2_desc">
              AWS Glue handles schema inference, cataloging, and PySpark-based transformations. The Glue Crawler keeps the Data Catalog updated when source schemas evolve. Glue jobs partition the output by date, making Athena queries efficient without full table scans.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-ink-800 transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d3_kicker">Query Layer</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d3_title">Athena — SQL on S3</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d3_desc">
              Athena makes the processed data queryable with standard SQL directly on S3 — no cluster to provision, no indexes to maintain, no database server running 24/7. Pay only per query scanned. For a data lake with infrequent ad-hoc queries, this is the right economics.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-ink-800 transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d4_kicker">Automation</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d4_title">GitHub Actions + Terraform CI/CD</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d4_desc">
              Every infrastructure change goes through a GitHub Actions workflow: Terraform plan runs on PR, apply runs on merge to main. No manual console changes. Every infrastructure change is code-reviewed, version-controlled, and auditable in git history.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ===== The Architecture ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-48">
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-16 items-start">
          <div class="md:col-span-5 md:sticky md:top-32">
            <h3 class="font-headline font-bold text-4xl mb-8 tracking-tight" data-i18n="blueprint_title">01 // The Architecture</h3>
            <p class="font-body text-lg text-on-surface-variant leading-loose mb-12" data-i18n="blueprint_desc">
              Kuiper is a fully serverless document intelligence pipeline. A user uploads a PDF via the React frontend to S3. A Step Functions state machine kicks off: Lambda triggers Textract for OCR, the extracted text chunks are indexed into a Bedrock Knowledge Base, and the document becomes queryable via a Nova Pro-powered chat interface.
            </p>
            <div class="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-secondary shrink-0"></span>
                <span class="font-label text-sm uppercase tracking-wider" data-i18n="bp_1">Step Functions State Machine Orchestration</span>
              </div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-primary shrink-0"></span>
                <span class="font-label text-sm uppercase tracking-wider" data-i18n="bp_2">Bedrock Knowledge Base + RAG</span>
              </div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-outline shrink-0"></span>
                <span class="font-label text-sm uppercase tracking-wider" data-i18n="bp_3">Terraform IaC — Zero Manual Setup</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="md:col-span-7">
            <div class="bg-ink-700 relative overflow-hidden flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-6 p-12" style="border-radius: 0.5rem; min-height: 400px;">
              <!-- Pipeline diagram -->
              <div class="flex items-center gap-3 flex-wrap justify-center">

      <!-- ===== The Challenge ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-48">
        <div class="bg-ink-700 p-8 md:p-24 relative overflow-hidden">
          <div class="absolute -top-12 -right-8 opacity-[0.04] font-headline font-black text-[10rem] select-none hidden md:block">RAG</div>
          <div class="max-w-3xl">
            <h3 class="font-headline font-bold text-4xl mb-12 tracking-tight" data-i18n="challenge_title">02 // The RAG Reliability Challenge</h3>
            <p class="font-body text-xl text-on-surface-variant leading-loose mb-16" data-i18n="challenge_desc">
              Building a document Q&amp;A system that actually answers questions from the document — and not from the model's training data — requires careful chunking strategy, embedding quality, and retrieval tuning. The Bedrock Knowledge Base handles embedding and vector search, but the chunking and prompt design that keeps answers grounded took real iteration.
            </p>
            <div class="code-insight">
              <div class="code-insight-header">
                <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-secondary text-sm">terminal</span>
                <span class="code-insight-label" data-i18n="insight_label">Engineering Insight</span>
              </div>
              <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant italic" data-i18n="insight_text">
                "The model knows everything. The hard part is making it answer only from your document — not from what it already knew."
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ===== Key Decisions ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-48">
        <h3 class="font-headline font-bold text-4xl mb-16 tracking-tight" data-i18n="decisions_title">03 // Key Engineering Decisions</h3>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-px bg-outline-variant/20">
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-ink-800 transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d1_kicker">Orchestration</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d1_title">Step Functions State Machine</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d1_desc">
              The ingestion pipeline — upload, OCR, chunk, embed, index — is orchestrated as a Step Functions state machine. Each step is a discrete Lambda. If Textract fails, only that step retries. The pipeline is observable, restartable, and decoupled.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-ink-800 transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d2_kicker">Document Intelligence</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d2_title">Textract for OCR</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d2_desc">
              AWS Textract handles multi-page PDFs including scanned documents and tables. Unlike basic PDF-to-text libraries, Textract preserves layout context and handles handwritten content — important for enterprise use cases with legacy document formats.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-ink-800 transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d3_kicker">AI Layer</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d3_title">Bedrock Knowledge Base + RAG</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d3_desc">
              Bedrock Knowledge Base manages the vector embeddings and similarity search. The chat Lambda retrieves relevant chunks and injects them into the Nova Pro prompt — grounding answers in the document. Hallucination is structurally constrained, not just prompted against.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-ink-800 transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d4_kicker">Infrastructure</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d4_title">Full Terraform IaC</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d4_desc">
              Every resource — S3 bucket, Lambda functions, Step Functions state machine, IAM roles, Bedrock Knowledge Base, API Gateway — is defined in Terraform. The entire stack stands up from zero with a single apply. No manual console configuration.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ===== WhatsApp Feedback ===== -->

          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-16">
            <div class="flex flex-col">
              <span class="metric-label" data-i18n="metric_1_label">Services</span>
              <h2 class="metric-value">3<span class="metric-unit"></span></h2>
              <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant mt-4 text-sm max-w-[220px]" data-i18n="metric_1_desc">Cart, Order, and Inventory — each a separate deployable service with its own persistence layer.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-col">
              <span class="metric-label" data-i18n="metric_2_label">Languages</span>
              <h2 class="metric-value">2<span class="metric-unit">+</span></h2>
              <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant mt-4 text-sm max-w-[220px]" data-i18n="metric_2_desc">C# (.NET 8) and Java (Spring Boot 3) backend services — plus React on the frontend.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-col">
              <span class="metric-label" data-i18n="metric_3_label">Infrastructure</span>
              <h2 class="metric-value text-5xl md:text-6xl" data-i18n="metric_3_value">Terraform</h2>
              <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant mt-4 text-sm max-w-[220px]" data-i18n="metric_3_desc">Full AWS deployment provisioned with Terraform — ECS, VPC, RDS, load balancers, IAM — from a single apply.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ===== The Architecture ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-48">
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-16 items-start">
          <div class="md:col-span-5 md:sticky md:top-32">
            <h3 class="font-headline font-bold text-4xl mb-8 tracking-tight" data-i18n="blueprint_title">01 // The Architecture</h3>
            <p class="font-body text-lg text-on-surface-variant leading-loose mb-12" data-i18n="blueprint_desc">
              StarMan is a polyglot microservices platform — each service is written in the language best suited to its domain, each runs in its own Docker container, and each has its own database. The React frontend talks to services via an API Gateway. Everything is deployed on AWS ECS Fargate with Terraform-managed infrastructure.
            </p>
            <div class="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-secondary shrink-0"></span>
                <span class="font-label text-sm uppercase tracking-wider" data-i18n="bp_1">Domain-Driven Service Boundaries</span>
              </div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-primary shrink-0"></span>
                <span class="font-label text-sm uppercase tracking-wider" data-i18n="bp_2">Per-Service Containerization (Docker)</span>
              </div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-outline shrink-0"></span>
                <span class="font-label text-sm uppercase tracking-wider" data-i18n="bp_3">AWS ECS Fargate + Terraform Deployment</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="md:col-span-7">
            <div class="bg-ink-700 relative overflow-hidden p-10" style="border-radius: 0.5rem; min-height: 400px;">
              <!-- Service cards -->
              <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4 mb-8">
                <div class="bg-ink-600 p-5 text-center" style="border-radius: 0.25rem;">
                  <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-secondary text-2xl block mb-2">shopping_cart</span>
                  <span class="font-label text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface block">CartService</span>
                  <span class="font-label text-[9px] text-cyan-arc opacity-70">C# .NET 8</span>
                </div>
                <div class="bg-ink-600 p-5 text-center" style="border-radius: 0.25rem;">
                  <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary text-2xl block mb-2">receipt_long</span>
                  <span class="font-label text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface block">OrderService</span>
                  <span class="font-label text-[9px] text-cyan-arc opacity-70">C# .NET 8</span>
                </div>
                <div class="bg-ink-600 p-5 text-center" style="border-radius: 0.25rem;">
                  <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-secondary text-2xl block mb-2">inventory_2</span>
                  <span class="font-label text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface block">InventoryService</span>
                  <span class="font-label text-[9px] text-cyan-arc opacity-70">Java Spring Boot 3</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="flex justify-center mb-8">
                <div class="bg-ink-600 px-8 py-4 text-center" style="border-radius: 0.25rem;">
                  <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-cyan-arc text-xl block mb-1">web</span>
                  <span class="font-label text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface">React Frontend</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="flex justify-center gap-4">
                <div class="bg-ink-600 px-6 py-3 text-center" style="border-radius: 0.25rem;">
                  <span class="font-label text-[9px] text-on-surface-variant uppercase tracking-wider">Docker · ECS Fargate · Terraform</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="absolute bottom-6 left-6 right-6 glass-effect p-4 border border-white/5" style="border-radius: 0.25rem;">
                <span class="font-label text-[10px] text-secondary font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-1" data-i18n="blueprint_overlay_label">Service Map</span>
                <p class="font-body text-xs text-on-surface-variant" data-i18n="blueprint_overlay_desc">React \u2192 API Gateway \u2192 CartService (.NET) · OrderService (.NET) · InventoryService (Java)</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ===== The Challenge ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-48">
        <div class="bg-ink-700 p-8 md:p-24 relative overflow-hidden">
          <div class="absolute -top-12 -right-8 opacity-[0.04] font-headline font-black text-[10rem] select-none hidden md:block">POLY</div>
          <div class="max-w-3xl">
            <h3 class="font-headline font-bold text-4xl mb-12 tracking-tight" data-i18n="challenge_title">02 // The Polyglot Challenge</h3>
            <p class="font-body text-xl text-on-surface-variant leading-loose mb-16" data-i18n="challenge_desc">
              Running multiple services in different languages on the same platform forces you to solve cross-cutting concerns — logging, error propagation, API contract consistency — without shared libraries. Each service team (even if it's one person) must treat the other services as external dependencies with documented contracts.
            </p>
            <div class="code-insight">
              <div class="code-insight-header">
                <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-secondary text-sm">terminal</span>
                <span class="code-insight-label" data-i18n="insight_label">Engineering Insight</span>
              </div>
              <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant italic" data-i18n="insight_text">
                "Polyglot microservices isn't about using every language — it's about using the right language for each domain, and owning the cost of that decision at the contract boundary."
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ===== Key Decisions ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-48">
        <h3 class="font-headline font-bold text-4xl mb-16 tracking-tight" data-i18n="decisions_title">03 // Key Engineering Decisions</h3>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-px bg-outline-variant/20">
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-ink-800 transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d1_kicker">Language Strategy</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d1_title">C# for Transactional, Java for Inventory</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d1_desc">
              C# .NET 8 handles Cart and Order — services with complex transactional logic where strong typing and the .NET ecosystem (Entity Framework, LINQ) shine. Java Spring Boot 3 handles Inventory — a domain with heavier query complexity where JPA and Spring Data offer proven patterns.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-ink-800 transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d2_kicker">Containerization</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d2_title">One Container per Service</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d2_desc">
              Each service ships as its own Docker image. This enforces isolation at the build level — no shared dependencies, no version conflicts between runtimes. ECS Fargate runs each container independently, and services can be scaled, updated, or replaced without touching the others.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-ink-800 transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d3_kicker">Data Isolation</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d3_title">Per-Service Persistence</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d3_desc">
              Each microservice owns its own database. No shared schema, no joins across service boundaries. Services communicate via API calls — not foreign keys. This makes each service independently deployable and prevents data coupling from sneaking back in through the database layer.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-ink-800 transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d4_kicker">Deployment</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d4_title">ECS Fargate + Terraform</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d4_desc">
              AWS ECS Fargate runs the containers without managing EC2 instances. Terraform provisions the full stack: VPC, subnets, security groups, ECS cluster, task definitions, ALB, RDS instances, and IAM roles. A single terraform apply brings the platform from zero to production.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

              <span class="metric-label" data-i18n="metric_3_label">Uptime</span>
              <h2 class="metric-value">99.9<span class="metric-unit">%</span></h2>
              <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant mt-4 text-sm max-w-[200px]" data-i18n="metric_3_desc">Production availability with zero-downtime deployments.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ===== The Architecture ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-48">
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-16 items-start">
          <div class="md:col-span-5 md:sticky md:top-32">
            <h3 class="font-headline font-bold text-4xl mb-8 tracking-tight" data-i18n="arch_title">02 // The Architecture</h3>
            <p class="font-body text-lg text-on-surface-variant leading-loose mb-12" data-i18n="arch_desc">
              SuperZAP is designed as a multi-tenant SaaS where each company operates in complete data isolation. The system handles real-time WhatsApp message routing, conversation assignment, and full CRM capabilities through a unified platform.
            </p>
            <div class="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-secondary"></span>
                <span class="font-label text-sm uppercase tracking-wider" data-i18n="arch_1">Real-Time WebSocket Messaging</span>
              </div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-primary"></span>
                <span class="font-label text-sm uppercase tracking-wider" data-i18n="arch_2">Multi-Tenant Data Isolation</span>
              </div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-outline"></span>
                <span class="font-label text-sm uppercase tracking-wider" data-i18n="arch_3">WhatsApp Business API Integration</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="md:col-span-7">
            <div class="bg-surface-container overflow-hidden" style="border-radius: 0.5rem;">
              <img src="/screenshots/32-operador-painel-contato.png" alt="Contact Panel" class="w-full h-auto" loading="lazy" />
            </div>
            <div class="mt-4 bg-surface-container overflow-hidden" style="border-radius: 0.5rem;">
              <img src="/screenshots/33-operador-scripts.png" alt="Quick Scripts Panel" class="w-full h-auto" loading="lazy" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ===== The Challenge ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-48">
        <div class="bg-surface-container-lowest p-8 md:p-24 relative overflow-hidden">
          <div class="absolute -top-12 -right-8 opacity-[0.05] font-headline font-black text-[10rem] select-none hidden md:block">SCALE</div>
          <div class="max-w-3xl">
            <h3 class="font-headline font-bold text-4xl mb-12 tracking-tight" data-i18n="challenge_title">03 // The Multi-Tenancy Challenge</h3>
	            <p class="font-body text-xl text-on-surface-variant leading-loose mb-16" data-i18n="challenge_desc">
	              Building a WhatsApp CRM for multiple tenants on a single codebase requires bulletproof tenant isolation, real-time message delivery, and the ability to handle message bursts without dropping conversations.
	            </p>
            <div class="code-insight">
              <div class="code-insight-header">
                <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-secondary text-sm">terminal</span>
                <span class="code-insight-label" data-i18n="insight_label">Engineering Insight</span>
              </div>
              <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant italic" data-i18n="insight_text">
                "Every tenant thinks they're the only user on the platform. That's the goal — complete isolation with shared infrastructure economics."
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ===== Key Decisions ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-48">
        <h3 class="font-headline font-bold text-4xl mb-16 tracking-tight" data-i18n="decisions_title">04 // Key Engineering Decisions</h3>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-px bg-outline-variant/20">
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-surface-container-low transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d1_kicker">Data Architecture</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d1_title">Tenant Scoping</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d1_desc">
              All database queries are scoped to the authenticated tenant via a mandatory tenant_id filter at the application layer. MySQL tables include tenant_id on every row — no cross-tenant data leakage is possible.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-surface-container-low transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d2_kicker">Real-Time Engine</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d2_title">WebSocket + Event Queue</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d2_desc">
              Messages arrive via WhatsApp webhook, enter a Redis-backed queue, and are delivered to operators in real-time via WebSocket. No polling, no missed messages.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-surface-container-low transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d3_kicker">Conversation Routing</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d3_title">Intelligent Assignment</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d3_desc">
              Conversations are automatically routed to available operators based on department, skills, and current load. Managers can reassign via the Kanban board in real-time.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-12 hover:bg-surface-container-low transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-6" data-i18n="d4_kicker">Operational Tooling</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-2xl mb-6" data-i18n="d4_title">Manager Dashboard</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed" data-i18n="d4_desc">
              Full operational visibility with live KPIs, agent performance metrics, lead pipeline tracking, and configurable reports. Managers control everything without touching code.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ===== Additional Screenshots ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-32">
