      <!-- ===== Recently Delivered ===== -->
      <section class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-8 mb-24 md:mb-48">
        <h3 class="font-headline font-bold text-4xl mb-4 tracking-tight" data-i18n="delivered_title">06 // Recently Delivered</h3>
        <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant mb-12 text-lg" data-i18n="delivered_desc">Shipped in the last 30 days and live in production.</p>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-px bg-outline-variant/20">
          <div class="bg-surface p-10 hover:bg-surface-container-low transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-4" data-i18n="del_1_kicker">Collaboration</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-xl mb-4" data-i18n="del_1_title">Operator-to-Operator Transfers</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant text-sm leading-relaxed" data-i18n="del_1_desc">Managers can transfer conversations between operators. The receiving agent sees a visual banner with a countdown and can return the conversation with a note.</p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-10 hover:bg-surface-container-low transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-4" data-i18n="del_2_kicker">Visibility</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-xl mb-4" data-i18n="del_2_title">Manager Notifications</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant text-sm leading-relaxed" data-i18n="del_2_desc">Real-time notification bell for managers. Receives alerts for help requests, conversation returns, and key operational events — with toast popups and unread badge.</p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-10 hover:bg-surface-container-low transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-4" data-i18n="del_3_kicker">Real-Time</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-xl mb-4" data-i18n="del_3_title">Presence Indicators</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant text-sm leading-relaxed" data-i18n="del_3_desc">Live contact status in the chat header: online, typing, recording audio, or last seen X minutes ago. Fetched on open, updated via WebSocket in real time.</p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-10 hover:bg-surface-container-low transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-4" data-i18n="del_4_kicker">Trust & Safety</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-xl mb-4" data-i18n="del_4_title">Blocked Numbers</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant text-sm leading-relaxed" data-i18n="del_4_desc">Block contacts from sending or receiving messages. Inbound messages are silently discarded at the webhook level — before any conversation is created.</p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-10 hover:bg-surface-container-low transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-4" data-i18n="del_5_kicker">Productivity</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-xl mb-4" data-i18n="del_5_title">Global Search</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant text-sm leading-relaxed" data-i18n="del_5_desc">Search across all conversations by contact name or message content. Accessible via Ctrl+K — results update as you type.</p>
          </div>
          <div class="bg-surface p-10 hover:bg-surface-container-low transition-colors duration-500">
            <span class="font-label text-primary text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block mb-4" data-i18n="del_6_kicker">Design</span>
            <h4 class="font-headline font-bold text-xl mb-4" data-i18n="del_6_title">Unified Design System</h4>
            <p class="font-body text-on-surface-variant text-sm leading-relaxed" data-i18n="del_6_desc">Consistent visual identity across all views with switchable themes. CSS token system shared between operator and manager interfaces.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
